-- ============================================================================
-- MÉTHODE TEE — V489.4.1
-- CATALOGUE CULINAIRE TEE VALIDÉ · 300 REPAS EN BASE ÉDITORIALE
-- + RÉSOLUTION CIQUAL SERVEUR · AUCUN ASSEMBLAGE LIBRE AU RUNTIME
--
-- À appliquer après V489.2.2. Ce patch est cumulatif pour la couche sémantique
-- V489.3.1 : il redéfinit mt_ciqual_role_v1 et mt_planner_recent_recommendations_v3.
--
-- Principes :
--   * les 3 585+ références CIQUAL restent visibles au cerveau ;
--   * elles ne sont plus combinées librement pour inventer un repas ;
--   * 300 repas éditoriaux sont chargés comme base de connaissance ;
--   * chaque composant est résolu côté serveur vers un code CIQUAL explicite ;
--   * seuls les repas dont TOUS les composants obligatoires sont résolus avec
--     forte confiance peuvent être publiés automatiquement ;
--   * les découvertes culturelles spécifiques (niveau 2) restent en revue
--     manuelle, sauf si une version whole-dish stricte existe déjà ailleurs ;
--   * aucune donnée CIQUAL, recette, protocole, paiement ou mémoire historique
--     n'est réécrite.
-- ============================================================================

begin;

do $preflight$
begin
  if to_regclass('public.ciqual_foods') is null
     or to_regclass('public.mt_planner_recommendation_history') is null
     or to_regprocedure('public.mt_planner_ciqual_universe_v1()') is null
     or to_regprocedure('public.mt_planner_ciqual_price_batch_v1(text[],text,text)') is null
     or to_regprocedure('public.mt_planner_recent_recommendations_v2(integer,integer)') is null
     or to_regprocedure('public.mt_planner_record_generation_v2(numeric,text,numeric,numeric,jsonb)') is null
     or to_regprocedure('public.mt_planner_candidate_meta_v2()') is null
     or to_regprocedure('public.mt_planner_purchase_quote_v2(jsonb,text)') is null
  then
    raise exception 'V48941_PREREQUIS_MANQUANT_APPLIQUER_V48922_AVANT';
  end if;
end
$preflight$;

-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 1) DURCISSEMENT SÉMANTIQUE V489.3.1 (cumulatif)
-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------
create or replace function public.mt_ciqual_role_v1(
  p_name text,
  p_kcal numeric,
  p_protein numeric,
  p_fat numeric,
  p_carbs numeric,
  p_fiber numeric
)
returns text
language plpgsql
immutable
as $fn$
declare
  t text:=public.food_normalize(coalesce(p_name,''));
  pr numeric:=coalesce(p_protein,0);
begin
  if t ~ '(^| )(eau|boisson|soda|jus|nectar|cafe|the|biere|vin|cidre|alcool)( |$)' then return 'beverage'; end if;
  if t ~ '(^| )(gateau|biscuit|bonbon|chocolat|glace|creme dessert|dessert|viennoiserie|croissant|tarte sucree)( |$)' then return 'sweet'; end if;
  if t ~ '(^| )(huile|beurre|margarine|mayonnaise|sauce|vinaigrette|sel|epice|epices|sucre|sirop|miel|condiment|vinaigre|cornichon|pickle|capre|bouillon)( |$)' then return 'condiment_fat'; end if;
  if t ~ '(^| )(graine|graines|semence|semences|ail|oignon|oignons|echalote|echalotes|basilic|persil|coriandre|ciboulette|menthe|aneth|thym|romarin|origan|estragon|herbe|herbes)( |$)' then return 'aromatic'; end if;
  if t ~ '(^| )(fruit|fruits|specialite de fruit|specialite de fruits|compote|confiture|pomme|poire|banane|orange|mandarine|clementine|citron|fraise|framboise|myrtille|raisin|mangue|ananas|kiwi|peche|abricot|prune|melon|pasteque|figue|datte)( |$)' then return 'fruit'; end if;
  if t ~ '(^| )(lait|yaourt|yaourts|fromage blanc|fromages blancs|skyr|skyrs|ricotta|mozzarella|feta|fromage|fromages|camembert|emmental|comte|brie|chevre)( |$)' then return 'dairy'; end if;
  if t ~ '(^| )(pizza|lasagne|lasagnes|couscous|paella|cassoulet|hachis|moussaka|choucroute|pot au feu|potee|bourguignon|chili|curry|tajine|risotto|ravioli|quiche|gratin|sandwich|burger|wrap|salade composee|plat compose|plat cuisine|soupe complete)( |$)' then return 'composite'; end if;
  if t ~ '(^| )(nugget|nuggets|croquette|croquettes|pane|panes|panee|panees|charcuterie|charcuteries|cordon bleu|cordons bleus|saucisse|saucisses|merguez|boudin|boudins|rillette|rillettes|terrine|terrines|quenelle|quenelles|hot dog|hot dogs|knack|knacks)( |$)' then return 'other'; end if;
  if t ~ '(^| )(lentille|lentilles|pois chiche|pois chiches|haricot blanc|haricots blancs|haricot rouge|haricots rouges|haricot noir|haricots noirs|flageolet|flageolets|tofu|tempeh|seitan)( |$)' then return 'protein_plant'; end if;
  if t ~ '(^| )(boeuf|veau|agneau|mouton|porc|jambon|poulet|dinde|canard|lapin|saumon|truite|thon|cabillaud|colin|merlu|lieu|dorade|bar|sardine|maquereau|hareng|anchois|crevette|crevettes|moule|moules|huitre|huitres|calamar|seiche|oeuf|oeufs)( |$)' and pr>=5 then return 'protein'; end if;
  if t ~ '(^| )(riz|pate|pates|spaghetti|nouille|nouilles|vermicelle|macaroni|semoule|couscous grain|boulgour|quinoa|pomme de terre|pommes de terre|patate douce|patates douces|pain|avoine|orge|millet|polenta|sarrasin|plantain)( |$)' then return 'starch'; end if;
  if t ~ '(^| )(courgette|courgettes|aubergine|aubergines|tomate|tomates|concombre|concombres|carotte|carottes|poivron|poivrons|brocoli|brocolis|chou|choux|epinard|epinards|salade|laitue|roquette|haricot vert|haricots verts|champignon|champignons|asperge|asperges|poireau|poireaux|betterave|betteraves|fenouil|celeri|navet|navets|courge|courges|potiron|potimarron|artichaut|artichauts|endive|endives|blette|blettes)( |$)' then return 'vegetable'; end if;
  return 'other';
end;
$fn$;

create or replace function public.mt_planner_recent_recommendations_v3(
  p_days integer default 42,
  p_generations integer default 4
)
returns jsonb
language sql
stable
security definer
set search_path=public
as $fn$
with p as (
  select auth.uid() uid,
         greatest(7,least(coalesce(p_days,42),180)) days,
         greatest(1,least(coalesce(p_generations,4),12)) gens
), h as (
  select r.*
  from public.mt_planner_recommendation_history r,p
  where r.user_id=p.uid
    and r.created_at>=now()-(p.days||' days')::interval
), by_candidate as (
  select candidate_id,max(candidate_title) candidate_title,
         max(nullif(candidate_signature,'')) candidate_signature,
         max(created_at) last_seen,
         count(*) filter(where created_at>=now()-interval '7 days')::int times_7d,
         count(*) filter(where created_at>=now()-interval '28 days')::int times_28d,
         count(*)::int times_window
  from h where candidate_id is not null and not is_leftover
  group by candidate_id
), by_signature as (
  select candidate_signature,max(candidate_title) candidate_title,max(created_at) last_seen,
         count(*) filter(where created_at>=now()-interval '7 days')::int times_7d,
         count(*) filter(where created_at>=now()-interval '28 days')::int times_28d,
         count(*)::int times_window
  from h where nullif(candidate_signature,'') is not null and not is_leftover
  group by candidate_signature
), generation_base as (
  select generation_id,max(created_at) created_at,max(budget_eur) budget_eur,max(budget_mode) budget_mode
  from h group by generation_id
), generation_rows as (
  select g.*,
    coalesce((select array_remove(array_agg(hh.candidate_id order by hh.day_index),null) from h hh where hh.generation_id=g.generation_id and not hh.is_leftover),'{}'::uuid[]) candidate_ids,
    coalesce((select array_remove(array_agg(hh.candidate_title order by hh.day_index),null) from h hh where hh.generation_id=g.generation_id and not hh.is_leftover),'{}'::text[]) candidate_titles,
    coalesce((select array_remove(array_agg(hh.candidate_signature order by hh.day_index),null) from h hh where hh.generation_id=g.generation_id and not hh.is_leftover),'{}'::text[]) candidate_signatures,
    coalesce((select array_agg(distinct u.component_key order by u.component_key)
              from h hh cross join lateral unnest(coalesce(hh.component_keys,'{}'::text[])) u(component_key)
              where hh.generation_id=g.generation_id and not hh.is_leftover and nullif(u.component_key,'') is not null),'{}'::text[]) component_keys
  from generation_base g
), recent_generations as (
  select g.* from generation_rows g order by g.created_at desc limit (select gens from p)
), component_counts as (
  select u.component_key,max(h.created_at) last_seen,
         count(*) filter(where h.created_at>=now()-interval '7 days')::int times_7d,
         count(*) filter(where h.created_at>=now()-interval '28 days')::int times_28d,
         count(*)::int times_window
  from h cross join lateral unnest(coalesce(h.component_keys,'{}'::text[])) u(component_key)
  where not h.is_leftover and nullif(u.component_key,'') is not null
  group by u.component_key
), stats as (
  select count(distinct generation_id)::int generations_window,
         count(distinct generation_id) filter(where week_key=date_trunc('week',current_date)::date)::int generations_this_week
  from h
)
select jsonb_build_object(
  'version','V48941_RECO_HISTORY_V3',
  'generations_window',coalesce(s.generations_window,0),
  'generations_this_week',coalesce(s.generations_this_week,0),
  'items',coalesce((select jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
    'candidate_id',candidate_id,'candidate_title',candidate_title,'candidate_signature',candidate_signature,
    'last_seen',last_seen,'times_7d',times_7d,'times_28d',times_28d,'times_window',times_window
  ) order by last_seen desc) from by_candidate),'[]'::jsonb),
  'signature_counts',coalesce((select jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
    'candidate_signature',candidate_signature,'candidate_title',candidate_title,'last_seen',last_seen,
    'times_7d',times_7d,'times_28d',times_28d,'times_window',times_window
  ) order by last_seen desc) from by_signature),'[]'::jsonb),
  'recent_generations',coalesce((select jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
    'generation_id',generation_id,'created_at',created_at,'budget_eur',budget_eur,'budget_mode',budget_mode,
    'candidate_ids',candidate_ids,'candidate_titles',candidate_titles,
    'candidate_signatures',candidate_signatures,'component_keys',component_keys
  ) order by created_at desc) from recent_generations),'[]'::jsonb),
  'component_counts',coalesce((select jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
    'component_key',component_key,'last_seen',last_seen,'times_7d',times_7d,'times_28d',times_28d,'times_window',times_window
  ) order by last_seen desc) from component_counts),'[]'::jsonb)
)
from stats s;
$fn$;

revoke all on function public.mt_planner_recent_recommendations_v3(integer,integer) from public,anon;
grant execute on function public.mt_planner_recent_recommendations_v3(integer,integer) to authenticated;

-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 2) SOURCE DE VÉRITÉ ÉDITORIALE V2 : REPAS + COMPOSANTS RÉSOLUS
-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------
create table if not exists public.mt_curated_meal_sources_v2(
  source_key text primary key,
  title text not null,
  publisher text not null,
  url text not null,
  usage_note text not null,
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

create table if not exists public.mt_curated_meals_v2(
  meal_code text primary key,
  title text not null,
  meal_kind text not null default 'composed_plate',
  country text,
  cuisine_family text not null,
  cultural_specific boolean not null default false,
  discovery_level smallint not null check(discovery_level between 0 and 2),
  meal_contexts text[] not null default array['lunch','dinner']::text[],
  diet_tags text[] not null default '{}'::text[],
  seasoning_note text,
  source_keys text[] not null,
  evidence_level text not null,
  auto_publish_policy text not null check(auto_publish_policy in (
    'safe_plate','canonical_mainstream','canonical_accessible','manual_specific','existing_strict_preferred'
  )),
  editorial_confidence numeric not null check(editorial_confidence between 0 and 1),
  editorial_note text,
  semantic_signature text not null unique,
  manual_reviewed boolean not null default false,
  review_status text not null default 'draft' check(review_status in ('draft','published','rejected','disabled')),
  enabled boolean not null default true,
  dataset_version text not null default 'V489.4.1',
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

create table if not exists public.mt_curated_meal_components_v2(
  component_id bigserial primary key,
  meal_code text not null references public.mt_curated_meals_v2(meal_code) on delete cascade,
  position smallint not null,
  component_name text not null,
  expected_role text not null,
  grams numeric not null check(grams>0 and grams<=800),
  lookup_terms text[] not null,
  required boolean not null default true,
  ciqual_code text,
  resolved_name text,
  resolved_role text,
  resolution_score numeric,
  second_score numeric,
  resolution_status text not null default 'unresolved' check(resolution_status in ('unresolved','resolved_medium','resolved_high','manual')),
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  unique(meal_code,position)
);

alter table public.mt_curated_meal_sources_v2 enable row level security;
alter table public.mt_curated_meals_v2 enable row level security;
alter table public.mt_curated_meal_components_v2 enable row level security;

drop policy if exists mt_curated_sources_v2_read on public.mt_curated_meal_sources_v2;
create policy mt_curated_sources_v2_read on public.mt_curated_meal_sources_v2
for select to authenticated using(true);

drop policy if exists mt_curated_meals_v2_read on public.mt_curated_meals_v2;
create policy mt_curated_meals_v2_read on public.mt_curated_meals_v2
for select to authenticated using(enabled and review_status='published');

drop policy if exists mt_curated_components_v2_read on public.mt_curated_meal_components_v2;
create policy mt_curated_components_v2_read on public.mt_curated_meal_components_v2
for select to authenticated using(
  exists(
    select 1 from public.mt_curated_meals_v2 m
    where m.meal_code=mt_curated_meal_components_v2.meal_code
      and m.enabled and m.review_status='published'
  )
);

  title=excluded.title,publisher=excluded.publisher,url=excluded.url,
  usage_note=excluded.usage_note,updated_at=now();

insert into public.mt_curated_meals_v2(
  meal_code,title,meal_kind,country,cuisine_family,cultural_specific,discovery_level,
  meal_contexts,diet_tags,seasoning_note,source_keys,evidence_level,auto_publish_policy,
  editorial_confidence,editorial_note,semantic_signature,enabled,dataset_version,review_status
)
select
  meal_code,title,meal_kind,nullif(country,''),cuisine_family,cultural_specific,discovery_level,
  meal_contexts,diet_tags,seasoning_note,source_keys,evidence_level,auto_publish_policy,
  editorial_confidence,editorial_note,semantic_signature,enabled,'V489.4.1','draft'
from src
on conflict(meal_code) do update set
  title=excluded.title,meal_kind=excluded.meal_kind,country=excluded.country,
  cuisine_family=excluded.cuisine_family,cultural_specific=excluded.cultural_specific,
  discovery_level=excluded.discovery_level,meal_contexts=excluded.meal_contexts,
  diet_tags=excluded.diet_tags,seasoning_note=excluded.seasoning_note,
  source_keys=excluded.source_keys,evidence_level=excluded.evidence_level,
  auto_publish_policy=excluded.auto_publish_policy,
  editorial_confidence=excluded.editorial_confidence,
  editorial_note=excluded.editorial_note,semantic_signature=excluded.semantic_signature,
  enabled=excluded.enabled,dataset_version='V489.4.1',review_status='draft',updated_at=now();

using public.mt_curated_meals_v2 m
where c.meal_code=m.meal_code and m.dataset_version='V489.4.1';

insert into public.mt_curated_meal_components_v2(
  meal_code,position,component_name,expected_role,grams,lookup_terms,required
)
select
  s.meal_code,
  ordinality::smallint,
  c->>'name',
  c->>'role',
  (c->>'grams')::numeric,
  coalesce(array(select jsonb_array_elements_text(c->'lookup_terms')),array[c->>'name']),
  true
from src s
cross join lateral jsonb_array_elements(s.components) with ordinality as z(c,ordinality);

-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 3) RÉSOLUTION CIQUAL FORTE ET EXPLICITE
-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------
create or replace function public.mt_curated_role_compatible_v2(
  p_expected text,
  p_actual text
)
returns boolean
language sql
immutable
as $fn$
select case
  when p_expected='protein' then p_actual='protein'
  when p_expected='protein_plant' then p_actual='protein_plant'
  when p_expected='dairy_protein' then p_actual='dairy'
  when p_expected='starch' then p_actual='starch'
  when p_expected='vegetable' then p_actual='vegetable'
  when p_expected='aromatic' then p_actual in ('aromatic','condiment_fat')
  when p_expected='sauce_component' then p_actual in ('protein_plant','condiment_fat','other')
  else false
end;
$fn$;

create or replace function public.mt_curated_component_match_v2(
  p_expected_role text,
  p_lookup_terms text[]
)
returns table(
  ciqual_code text,
  resolved_name text,
  resolved_role text,
  match_score numeric,
  second_score numeric
)
language sql
stable
security definer
set search_path=public
as $fn$
with terms as (
  select distinct public.food_normalize(t) term
  from unnest(coalesce(p_lookup_terms,'{}'::text[])) t
  where nullif(btrim(t),'') is not null
), universe as (
  select
    u.ciqual_code,
    u.name,
    u.display_name,
    u.role,
    public.food_normalize(coalesce(u.display_name,u.name)) dname,
    public.food_normalize(u.name) uname
  from public.mt_planner_ciqual_universe_v1() u
  where public.mt_curated_role_compatible_v2(p_expected_role,u.role)
), scored as (
  select u.ciqual_code,u.name,u.display_name,u.role,
    max(
      case
        when u.uname=t.term or u.dname=t.term then 100::numeric
        when u.uname like '%'||t.term||'%' or u.dname like '%'||t.term||'%' then 97::numeric
        else (
          select case
            when count(*) filter(where length(tok)>=3)=0 then 0::numeric
            when avg(case when u.uname like '%'||tok||'%' or u.dname like '%'||tok||'%' then 1.0 else 0.0 end)
                 filter(where length(tok)>=3) >= 0.999 then 93::numeric
            when avg(case when u.uname like '%'||tok||'%' or u.dname like '%'||tok||'%' then 1.0 else 0.0 end)
                 filter(where length(tok)>=3) >= 0.80 then 88::numeric
            when avg(case when u.uname like '%'||tok||'%' or u.dname like '%'||tok||'%' then 1.0 else 0.0 end)
                 filter(where length(tok)>=3) >= 0.66 then 82::numeric
            else 0::numeric
          end
          from regexp_split_to_table(t.term,E'\\s+') tok
        )
      end
    ) score
  from universe u
  cross join terms t
  group by u.ciqual_code,u.name,u.display_name,u.role
), ranked as (
  select s.*,row_number() over(order by score desc,length(coalesce(display_name,name)) asc,ciqual_code asc) rn,
         lead(score) over(order by score desc,length(coalesce(display_name,name)) asc,ciqual_code asc) next_score
  from scored s
  where score>0
)
select
  r.ciqual_code,
  coalesce(r.display_name,r.name),
  r.role,
  r.score,
  coalesce(r.next_score,0)
from ranked r
where r.rn=1;
$fn$;

revoke all on function public.mt_curated_component_match_v2(text,text[]) from public,anon;
grant execute on function public.mt_curated_component_match_v2(text,text[]) to authenticated;

do $resolve$
declare
  c record;
  m record;
begin
  for c in
    select component_id,expected_role,lookup_terms
    from public.mt_curated_meal_components_v2 cc
    join public.mt_curated_meals_v2 mm using(meal_code)
    where mm.dataset_version='V489.4.1'
  loop
    select * into m
    from public.mt_curated_component_match_v2(c.expected_role,c.lookup_terms);

    if m.ciqual_code is null then
      update public.mt_curated_meal_components_v2
      set ciqual_code=null,resolved_name=null,resolved_role=null,
          resolution_score=null,second_score=null,resolution_status='unresolved',updated_at=now()
      where component_id=c.component_id;
    elsif m.match_score>=97
       or (m.match_score>=93 and (m.match_score-coalesce(m.second_score,0))>=3)
    then
      update public.mt_curated_meal_components_v2
      set ciqual_code=m.ciqual_code,resolved_name=m.resolved_name,resolved_role=m.resolved_role,
          resolution_score=m.match_score,second_score=m.second_score,
          resolution_status='resolved_high',updated_at=now()
      where component_id=c.component_id;
    elsif m.match_score>=88 then
      update public.mt_curated_meal_components_v2
      set ciqual_code=m.ciqual_code,resolved_name=m.resolved_name,resolved_role=m.resolved_role,
          resolution_score=m.match_score,second_score=m.second_score,
          resolution_status='resolved_medium',updated_at=now()
      where component_id=c.component_id;
    else
      update public.mt_curated_meal_components_v2
      set ciqual_code=null,resolved_name=null,resolved_role=null,
          resolution_score=m.match_score,second_score=m.second_score,
          resolution_status='unresolved',updated_at=now()
      where component_id=c.component_id;
    end if;
  end loop;
end
$resolve$;

-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 4) PUBLICATION CONSERVATRICE
-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------
update public.mt_curated_meals_v2
set review_status=case
      when not enabled or auto_publish_policy='existing_strict_preferred' then 'disabled'
      else 'draft'
    end,
    updated_at=now()
where dataset_version='V489.4.1';

update public.mt_curated_meals_v2 m
set review_status='published',updated_at=now()
where m.dataset_version='V489.4.1'
  and m.enabled
  and m.auto_publish_policy in ('safe_plate','canonical_mainstream','canonical_accessible')
  and m.editorial_confidence>=0.86
  and (m.discovery_level<2 or m.manual_reviewed)
  and cardinality(m.source_keys)>=2
  and not exists(
    select 1
    from unnest(m.source_keys) k
    left join public.mt_curated_meal_sources_v2 s on s.source_key=k
    where s.source_key is null
  )
  and exists(
    select 1 from public.mt_curated_meal_components_v2 c
    where c.meal_code=m.meal_code and c.expected_role in ('protein','protein_plant','dairy_protein')
  )
  and exists(
    select 1 from public.mt_curated_meal_components_v2 c
    where c.meal_code=m.meal_code and c.expected_role='starch'
  )
  and exists(
    select 1 from public.mt_curated_meal_components_v2 c
    where c.meal_code=m.meal_code and c.expected_role='vegetable'
  )
  and not exists(
    select 1 from public.mt_curated_meal_components_v2 c
    where c.meal_code=m.meal_code
      and c.required
      and c.resolution_status<>'resolved_high'
  )
  and not exists(
    select 1 from public.mt_curated_meal_components_v2 c
    where c.meal_code=m.meal_code
      and public.food_normalize(c.component_name) ~ '(^| )(nugget|nuggets|croquette|croquettes|charcuterie|charcuteries|cordon bleu|cordons bleus|saucisse|saucisses|boudin|boudins|rillette|rillettes|terrine|terrines|quenelle|quenelles)( |$)'
  );

-- Les niveaux culturels spécifiques restent volontairement en draft tant qu'un
-- humain n'a pas posé manual_reviewed=true. Cela n'empêche pas les versions
-- whole-dish strictes V488.7/V488.8 déjà existantes d'être proposées.

create or replace function public.mt_planner_curated_catalog_v2()
returns table(
  meal_code text,
  title text,
  meal_kind text,
  country text,
  cuisine_family text,
  cultural_specific boolean,
  discovery_level smallint,
  meal_contexts text[],
  diet_tags text[],
  components jsonb,
  seasoning_note text,
  source_keys text[],
  evidence_level text,
  semantic_signature text,
  editorial_confidence numeric
)
language sql
stable
security definer
set search_path=public
as $fn$
select
  m.meal_code,m.title,m.meal_kind,m.country,m.cuisine_family,m.cultural_specific,
  m.discovery_level,m.meal_contexts,m.diet_tags,
  coalesce((
    select jsonb_agg(
      jsonb_build_object(
        'name',c.component_name,
        'role',c.expected_role,
        'grams',c.grams,
        'ciqual_code',c.ciqual_code,
        'resolved_name',c.resolved_name,
        'resolved_role',c.resolved_role,
        'resolution_score',c.resolution_score
      )
      order by c.position
    )
    from public.mt_curated_meal_components_v2 c
    where c.meal_code=m.meal_code
  ),'[]'::jsonb) components,
  m.seasoning_note,m.source_keys,m.evidence_level,m.semantic_signature,m.editorial_confidence
from public.mt_curated_meals_v2 m
where m.enabled and m.review_status='published'
order by m.meal_code;
$fn$;

revoke all on function public.mt_planner_curated_catalog_v2() from public,anon;
grant execute on function public.mt_planner_curated_catalog_v2() to authenticated;

create or replace function public.mt_curated_catalog_audit_v2()
returns jsonb
language sql
stable
security definer
set search_path=public
as $fn$
select jsonb_build_object(
  'version','V489.4.1',
  'meals_total',(select count(*) from public.mt_curated_meals_v2 where dataset_version='V489.4.1'),
  'published',(select count(*) from public.mt_curated_meals_v2 where dataset_version='V489.4.1' and review_status='published'),
  'draft',(select count(*) from public.mt_curated_meals_v2 where dataset_version='V489.4.1' and review_status='draft'),
  'disabled',(select count(*) from public.mt_curated_meals_v2 where dataset_version='V489.4.1' and review_status='disabled'),
  'level_0',(select count(*) from public.mt_curated_meals_v2 where dataset_version='V489.4.1' and discovery_level=0),
  'level_1',(select count(*) from public.mt_curated_meals_v2 where dataset_version='V489.4.1' and discovery_level=1),
  'level_2',(select count(*) from public.mt_curated_meals_v2 where dataset_version='V489.4.1' and discovery_level=2),
  'components_total',(select count(*) from public.mt_curated_meal_components_v2 c join public.mt_curated_meals_v2 m using(meal_code) where m.dataset_version='V489.4.1'),
  'components_resolved_high',(select count(*) from public.mt_curated_meal_components_v2 c join public.mt_curated_meals_v2 m using(meal_code) where m.dataset_version='V489.4.1' and c.resolution_status='resolved_high'),
  'components_resolved_medium',(select count(*) from public.mt_curated_meal_components_v2 c join public.mt_curated_meals_v2 m using(meal_code) where m.dataset_version='V489.4.1' and c.resolution_status='resolved_medium'),
  'components_unresolved',(select count(*) from public.mt_curated_meal_components_v2 c join public.mt_curated_meals_v2 m using(meal_code) where m.dataset_version='V489.4.1' and c.resolution_status='unresolved'),
  'manual_specific_waiting',(select count(*) from public.mt_curated_meals_v2 where dataset_version='V489.4.1' and auto_publish_policy='manual_specific' and review_status='draft')
);
$fn$;

revoke all on function public.mt_curated_catalog_audit_v2() from public,anon;
grant execute on function public.mt_curated_catalog_audit_v2() to authenticated;

commit;

select jsonb_build_object(
  'status','v48941_catalogue_culinaire_tee_valide_backend_pret',
  'counts',jsonb_build_object(
    'loaded',(select count(*) from public.mt_curated_meals_v2 where dataset_version='V489.4.1'),
    'published',(select count(*) from public.mt_curated_meals_v2 where dataset_version='V489.4.1' and review_status='published'),
    'draft',(select count(*) from public.mt_curated_meals_v2 where dataset_version='V489.4.1' and review_status='draft'),
    'disabled',(select count(*) from public.mt_curated_meals_v2 where dataset_version='V489.4.1' and review_status='disabled'),
    'level_0',(select count(*) from public.mt_curated_meals_v2 where dataset_version='V489.4.1' and discovery_level=0),
    'level_1',(select count(*) from public.mt_curated_meals_v2 where dataset_version='V489.4.1' and discovery_level=1),
    'level_2',(select count(*) from public.mt_curated_meals_v2 where dataset_version='V489.4.1' and discovery_level=2),
    'components_high',(select count(*) from public.mt_curated_meal_components_v2 c join public.mt_curated_meals_v2 m using(meal_code) where m.dataset_version='V489.4.1' and c.resolution_status='resolved_high'),
    'components_medium',(select count(*) from public.mt_curated_meal_components_v2 c join public.mt_curated_meals_v2 m using(meal_code) where m.dataset_version='V489.4.1' and c.resolution_status='resolved_medium'),
    'components_unresolved',(select count(*) from public.mt_curated_meal_components_v2 c join public.mt_curated_meals_v2 m using(meal_code) where m.dataset_version='V489.4.1' and c.resolution_status='unresolved')
  ),
  'architecture',jsonb_build_object(
    'curated_catalog_v2_rpc',to_regprocedure('public.mt_planner_curated_catalog_v2()') is not null,
    'catalog_audit_v2_rpc',to_regprocedure('public.mt_curated_catalog_audit_v2()') is not null,
    'semantic_rotation_v3',to_regprocedure('public.mt_planner_recent_recommendations_v3(integer,integer)') is not null,
    'component_ciqual_codes_materialized',true,
    'free_ciqual_assembly_required',false
  ),
  'guards',jsonb_build_object(
    'all_300_auto_published',false,
    'level_2_requires_manual_review',true,
    'existing_strict_duplicates_disabled',true,
    'strong_component_resolution_required',true,
    'unknown_price_becomes_zero',false,
    'ciqual_rows_modified',false,
    'external_ai',false
  ),
  'unchanged',jsonb_build_object(
    'recipes',true,'ciqual_rows',true,'food_dictionary',true,'prices',true,
    'payments',true,'protocols',true,'voice',true,'historical_recommendations',true
  ),
  'next_step','Envoyer ce JSON. Si curated_catalog_v2_rpc=true, uploader les 4 fichiers frontend V489.4.1 et tester le planner.'
) as v48941_result;
